    主席報告



    Dear Shareholders,                                                  各位股東：


    On behalf of the Board of Consun Pharmaceutical Group               本人謹此代表康臣葯業集團有限公司董事局，提
    Limited, I am pleased to present the audited consolidated           呈本集團截至2025 年12 月31 日止年度經審核之
    results of the Group for the year ended 31 December 2025.           綜合業績。


    I.    Business Performance: Synergistic development in              一、   業務表現：院內院外協同發力，創新與市
          and out of hospitals, driven by the dual engines of                場雙輪驅動
          innovation and market expansion

          In 2025, the Chinese pharmaceutical industry entered               2025年，中國醫藥行業邁入深度調整期，
          a phase of profound adjustment, characterized by the               政策、技術與市場的三重變革交織，既帶
          intertwined triple transformations in policy, technology,          來挑戰，亦孕育機遇。政策端，《中華人
          and market, posing challenges as well as breeding                  民共和國藥典（2025 年版）》正式實施，
          opportunities. On the policy front, the Pharmacopoeia              藥品全生命週期質量管理標準全面對標國
          of the People’s Republic of China (2025 Edition) was               際，集採規則持續優化、質量監管強化與
          officially implemented, comprehensive life-cycle quality           價格治理深化，推動行業從「規模擴張」向
          management standards for drugs fully aligned with                  「價值創造」轉型；技術端，AI與數字化已
          international standards, and the continuous optimization           從「可選工具」變為「必選項」—— 從研發
          of centralized procurement rules, strengthening of                 靶點篩選到生產智能管控，再到營銷患者
          quality supervision, and deepening of price governance             服務，全產業鏈數字化部署成為企業核心
          promoted the industry’s transformation from “scale                 競爭力；市場端，行業整體增速雖趨緩，
          expansion” to “value creation”; on the technology front,           但結構性機會凸顯：具備臨床價值、不可
          AI and digitalization transformed from “optional tools” to         替代性及創新能力的藥品與服務，仍是增
          “mandatory requirements” – from R&D target screening               長的核心驅動力。
          to intelligent production control, and further to marketing
          and patient services, the digital deployment across the
          entire industry chain became the core competitiveness
          of enterprises; on the market front, although the overall
          growth rate of the industry slowed down, structural
          opportunities became prominent: drugs and services
          with clinical value, irreplaceability, and innovation
          capabilities remained the core driving forces for growth.

design” to “implementation”, with its daily operations     先地位進一步鞏固，核心產品市場份額穩
becoming increasingly sophisticated. The Group             步提升，盈利能力持續增強，展現出穩健

of core products alongside continuously improved           幣10.8億元，同比增長約18.4%，以紥實

development. In 2025, the Group recorded sales

18.4%, confirming the effectiveness of the strategy with
solid results.

     TREASURY POLICIES                                                  庫務政策
     The Group has adopted a prudent financial management               本集團在執行庫務政策上採取審慎的財務管理策
     approach towards its treasury policies and thus maintained         略，因此於2025年內維持健全的流動資金狀況。
     a healthy liquidity position throughout the year of 2025. The      本集團不斷審核及評估客戶的信貸狀況及財務狀
     Group strives to reduce exposure to credit risk by performing      況，務求降低信貸風險。為管理流動資金風險，
     ongoing credit assessments and evaluations of the financial        董事局密切監察本集團的流動資金狀況，以確保
     status of its customers. To manage liquidity risk, the Board       本集團的資產、負債及其他承擔的流動結構能應
     closely monitors the Group’s liquidity position to ensure that     付不時的資金需要。本集團的現金及現金等值物
     the liquidity structure of the Group’s assets, liabilities and     主要以人民幣及港元計值。
     other commitments can meet its funding requirements from
     time to time. Cash and cash equivalents of the Group are
     mainly denominated in RMB and HKD.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE                                               企業管治
The Group’s business philosophy is “Based on principle,            本集團的經營哲學是「以道為本、以德為基、以
founded on morality, achieving benefits while prioritizing         義為上、義利共生」，其中「以義為上，義利共
righteousness”, among which, the value of “achieving benefits      生」的義利觀是一個非常重要的組成部分。以義
while prioritizing rightness” is a very important component.       為上，強調「見利思義」和「思利及人」，即堅持以
We insist and emphasize on the priority of righteousness in        義為上，以義為先，以義取利，合義取利，不取
our operations, and gain benefits from our righteous and           無義之利。義利共生，強調義利不可分割，無利
ethical actions, while never take any benefits from unrighteous    則組織和個人都難以生存和發展，當義與利發生
actions. Righteousness and benefits are inseparable. Neither       矛盾時，先義後利，義利並舉。
can organizations nor individuals would survive and develop
without economic benefits, but when conflicts happen
between righteousness and benefits, we always prioritize
righteousness and achieve a win-win situation that we can
pursue righteousness and economic benefits at the same time.


Adapting and adhering to recognised standards of corporate         配合及遵守企業管治原則及常規之公認標準亦為
governance principles and practices is also the top priorities     本公司最優先原則。董事局相信良好的企業管治
of the Company. The Board believes that good corporate             能引領本公司走向成功及平衡股東、客戶以及僱
governance could lead the Company to success and balance           員間之利益，因此董事局致力於持續檢討及改善
the interests of shareholders, customers and employees,            遵守該等原則及常規之效率及有效性。
and the Board is therefore devoted to ongoing reviews
and enhancements of the efficiency and effectiveness of
compliance with such principles and practices.

BOARD DIVERSITY POLICY                                             董事局多元化政策
The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of having         本公司明白並深信董事局成員多元化對提升其表
a diverse Board to enhance the quality of its performance. With    現素質裨益良多。為達致可持續及均衡的發展，
a view to achieving a sustainable and balanced development,        本公司視董事局層面日益多元化為支持其達到戰
the Company sees increasing diversity at the Board level           略目標及維持可持續發展的關鍵元素，並已採納
as an essential element in supporting the attainment of its        董事局多元化政策。
strategic objectives and its sustainable development, and has
adopted the Board Diversity Policy.

     All Board appointments will be based on meritocracy, and           董事局所有委任均將以用人唯才為原則，並在考
     candidates will be considered against appropriate criteria,        慮人選時以適當的準則充分顧及董事局成員多元
     having due regard for the benefits of diversity on the Board.      化的裨益。甄選人選將按一系列多元化範疇為基
     Selection of candidates will be based on a range of diversity      準，除教育背景、專業經驗、技能、知識及服務
     perspectives, including but not limited to gender, age,            任期外，亦包括但不限於性別、年齡、文化背景
     cultural background and ethnicity, in addition to educational      及種族。最終將按人選的長處及可為董事局提供
     background, professional experience, skills, knowledge and         的貢獻而作出決定。
     length of service. The ultimate decision will be based on merit
     and contribution that the selected candidates will bring to the
     Board.

                                                              including future revenue growth rates, future gross
                                                              margins and the discount rate applied in the
                                                              discounted cash flow forecast and considering
                                                              the resulting impact on the impairment testing and
                                                              whether there was any indicator of management bias
                                                              in the selection of these key assumptions; and
                                                          •   對關鍵假設（包括未來收入增長率、未來毛利率及折現
                                                              現金流量預測中應用的折現率）進行敏感度分析，並考
                                                              慮由此對減值測試產生的影響及挑選該等關鍵假設時是
                                                              否存在任何管理層偏見的跡象；及


                                                          •   considering the reasonableness of the disclosures in
                                                              the financial statements in respect of management’s
                                                              impairment assessments of intangible assets –
                                                              trademark allocated to the Relevant CGU with
                                                              reference to the requirements of the prevailing
                                                              accounting standards.
                                                          •   參考現行會計準則的規定，考慮財務報表中有關管理層
                                                              對分配至相關現金產生單位的無形資產 － 商標進行減值
                                                              評估的披露資料的合理性。

    (d)   Subsidiaries and non-controlling interests                     (d)   附屬公司及非控股權益

          Subsidiaries are entities controlled by the                          附屬公司指本集團控制的實體。本
          Group. The Group controls an entity when it is                       集團可以或有權從參與實體的業務
          exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from                     分享非固定回報，且有能力行使對

    (i)   Research and development costs                                 (i)   研發成本

          and development activities or that can be                            動的成本。基於本集團研發活動的
          allocated on a reasonable basis to such                              性質，該等成本一般要直至項目較

          for the recognition of such costs as an asset are                    產。因此，研究成本及開發成本一
          generally not met until late in the development                      般均於其產生的期間確認為開支。
          stage of the project when the remaining
          development costs are immaterial. Hence both
          research costs and development costs are
          generally recognised as expenses in the period
          in which they are incurred.

                 Write-off policy                                                       撇銷政策

                 The gross carrying amount of a financial                                倘並無實際可收回的前景，
                 asset or lease receivable is written off                                金融資產或租賃應收款項的
                 to the extent that there is no realistic                                賬面總額會被撇銷。該情況
                 prospect of recovery. This is generally the                             一般在本集團釐定債務人並
                 case when the Group determines that the                                 無資產或收入來源可產生足
                 debtor does not have assets or sources of                               夠的現金流以償還將予撇銷
                 income that could generate sufficient cash                              的款項時出現。
                 flows to repay the amounts subject to the
                 write-off.


                 Subsequent recoveries of an asset that                                  倘過往已撇銷的資產於其後
                 was previously written off are recognised                               收回，則於收回期間於損益
                 as a reversal of impairment in profit or loss                           確認為減值撥回。
                 in the period in which the recovery occurs.


          (ii)   Impairment of other non-current assets                         (ii)    其他非流動資產減值

                 At each reporting date, the Group reviews                               於各個報告日期，本集團
                 the carrying amounts of its non-financial                               審閱其非金融資產（存貨及
                 assets (other than inventories and deferred                             遞延稅項資產除外）的賬面
                 tax assets) to determine whether there                                  值，以釐定是否存在任何減
                 is any indication of impairment. If any                                 值跡象。倘出現任何該等跡
                 such indication exists, then the asset’s                                象，則會估計資產的可收回
                 recoverable amount is estimated. Goodwill                               金額。商譽每年進行減值測
                 is tested annually for impairment.                                      試。


                 For impairment testing, assets are                                      就減值測試而言，資產被分
                 grouped together into the smallest                                      類成最小資產組別，有關資
                 group of assets that generates cash                                     產組別透過持續使用產生現
                 inflows from continuing use that are                                    金流入，且基本上獨立於其
                 largely independent of the cash inflows                                 他資產或現金產生單位（「現
                 of other assets or cash-generating                                      金產生單位」）的現金流入。
                 units (“CGU”s). Goodwill arising from a                                 業務合併所產生的商譽分配
                 business combination is allocated to CGUs                               至現金產生單位或現金產生
                 or groups of CGUs that are expected                                     單位組別，有關組別預計將
                 to benefit from the synergies of the                                    從合併的協同效應中受益。
                 combination.

    (m)   Inventories                                                         (m)   存貨

          and condition.

          costs necessary to make the sale.

                                  applying the effective interest rate                            資產不再出現信貸減
                                  to the amortised cost of the financial                          值，利息收益將恢復
                                  asset. If the asset is no longer                                至以總額基準計算。
                                  credit-impaired, then the calculation
                                  of interest income reverts to the
                                  gross basis.

                           of the respective subsidiaries. This                            惟 不 可 用 於 分 派（ 清 盤 除

                           accumulated losses or increasing capital

     (Continued)
     (f)   Capital management                                                           (f)   資本管理

           The Group’s primary objectives when managing                                       本集團管理資本的主要目的是維護
           capital are to safeguard the Group’s ability to                                    其持續經營的能力，從而使其能夠
           continue as a going concern, so that it can                                        透過按相應的風險水平為產品及服
           continue to provide returns for shareholders                                       務定價，以及憑藉以合理成本取得
           and benefits for other stakeholders, by pricing                                    融資而繼續為股東提供回報，並為
           products and services commensurately with the                                      其他利益相關者提供利益。
           level of risk and by securing access to finance at
           a reasonable cost.


           The Group actively and regularly reviews and                                       本集團積極定期檢討及管理其資本
           manages its capital structure to maintain a                                        結構，以維持較高借貸水平可能帶

           afforded by a sound capital position, and makes                                    行調整。
           adjustments to the capital structure in light of
           changes in economic conditions.


           The Group monitors its capital structure on the                                    本集團按照經調整淨債務與資本比

           For this purpose, adjusted net debt is defined                                     經調整淨債務乃界定為總債務（包

           dividends, less cash and cash equivalents and                                      以及原到期日超過三個月的銀行存

           over three months. Adjusted capital comprises all                                  成部分。
           components of equity.

29   FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT AND FAIR                                 29     財務風險管理及金融工具的公允值
     VALUES OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS
     Exposure to credit, liquidity, interest rate and currency                 本集團須面對於日常業務過程中產生的信
     risks arises in the normal course of the Group’s                          貸、流動資金、利率及貨幣風險。本集團
     business. The Group’s exposure to these risks and the                     所面對的該等風險及本集團用以管理該等
     financial risk management policies and practices used                     風險的財務風險管理政策及慣例載述如
     by the Group to manage these risks are described                          下。
     below.


     (a)      Credit risk                                                      (a)   信貸風險

              Credit risk refers to the risk that a counterparty                     信貸風險指交易對手未履行其合約
              will default on its contractual obligations resulting                  責任而導致本集團產生財務損失的
              in a financial loss to the Group. The Group’s                          風險。本集團的信貸風險主要來自
              credit risk is primarily attributable to trade                         貿易應收款項及其他應收款項。
              receivables and other receivables. The Group’s                         本集團自現金及現金等值項目、應
              exposure to credit risk arising from cash and                          收票據及應收利息所產生的信貸風
              cash equivalents, bills receivable and interest                        險有限，因為交易對手為銀行及金
              receivables is limited because the counterparties                      融機構，本集團認為其信貸風險偏
              are banks and financial institutions, for which                        低。管理層制定了信貸政策，並對
              the Group considers to represent low credit risk.                      該等信貸風險進行持續監控。
              Management has a credit policy in place and the
              exposures to these credit risks are monitored on
              an ongoing basis.


           Trade receivables                                                         貿易應收款項

              The Group has established a credit risk management                     本集團已設立信貸風險管理政策，
              policy under which individual credit evaluations are                   據此對所有要求授予一定金額信貸
              performed on all customers requiring credit over                       的客戶進行個別信貸評估。該等評
              a certain amount. These evaluations focus on the                       估著重客戶過往到期還款記錄以及
              customer’s past history of making payments when                        當前的支付能力，並會考慮關於客
              due and current ability to pay and take into account                   戶以及客戶經營所處經濟環境的特
              information specific to the customer as well as                        定資料。貿易應收款項自發出賬單
              pertaining to the economic environment in which the                    日期起計90日內到期。一般而言，
              customer operates. Trade receivables are due within                    本集團不會收取客戶提供的抵押
              90 days from the date of billing. Normally, the Group                  品。
              does not obtain collateral from customers.

           adopted for the foreign exchange transactions
           are the rates of exchange quoted by the People’s
           Bank of China that are determined largely by
           supply and demand.

           are denominated in a foreign currency, i.e. a                        款，即與交易相關但並非經營業務

           operations to which the transactions relate. The                     的貨幣主要為港元及美元。
           currencies giving rise to this risk are primarily

     (e)   Fair value measurement                                          (e)   公允值計量

           (i)     Financial assets and liabilities measured                     (i)    按公允值計量的金融資產及
                   at fair value                                                        負債

                   Fair value hierarchy                                                 公允值層級

                   The following table presents the fair                                 下表呈列本集團於報告期末
                   value of the Group’s financial instruments                            按經常性基準計量的金融工
                   measured at the end of the reporting period                           具公允值，有關公允值於香
                   on a recurring basis, categorised into the                            港財務報告準則第13 號「公
                   three-level fair value hierarchy as defined                           允值計量」所界定的公允值
                   in HKFRS 13, Fair value measurement . The                             三級架構中進行分類。公允
                   level into which a fair value measurement                             值所歸類的層級乃經參考以
                   is classified is determined with reference                            下估值方法所用的輸入數據
                   to the observability and significance of the                          的可觀察性及重要性而釐
                   inputs used in the valuation technique as                             定：
                   follows:


                   •      Level 1 valuations:   Fair value measured using only Level 1 inputs i.e. unadjusted
                                                quoted prices in active markets for identical assets or liabilities
                                                at the measurement date
                   •      第一級估值：                僅使用第一級輸入數據（即於計量日期相同資產或負債於活躍市場的
                                                未經調整報價）計量的公允值

                   •      Level 2 valuations:   Fair value measured using Level 2 inputs i.e. observable
                                                inputs which fail to meet Level 1, and not using significant
                                                unobservable inputs. Unobservable inputs are inputs for which
                                                market data are not available
                   •      第二級估值：                使用第二級輸入數據（即未能達到第一級的可觀察輸入數據）且並非
                                                使用重大不可觀察輸入數據計量的公允值。不可觀察輸入數據為無法
                                                取得市場數據的輸入數據

                   •      Level 3 valuations:   Fair value measured using significant unobservable inputs
                   •      第三級估值：                使用重大不可觀察輸入數據計量的公允值

     HKFRS 18 will replace HKAS 1 Presentation of financial               香港財務報告準則第18號將取代香港會計
     statements and aims to improve the transparency and                  準則第1 號「財務報表的呈列」，旨在提高
     comparability of information about an entity’s financial             實體財務報表資訊的透明度及可比性。香
     statements. HKFRS 18 is effective for annual reporting               港財務報告準則第18 號於2027 年1 月1 日
     periods beginning on or after 1 January 2027 and is to               或以後開始的年度報告期間生效，並需追
     be applied retrospectively.                                          溯應用。


     Among other changes, under HKFRS 18, entities are                    除其他變動外，根據香港財務報告準則第
     required to classify all income and expenses into five               18 號，實體須將損益表中的所有收入及
     categories in the statement of profit or loss, namely the            開支分類為五個類別，即經營、投資、融
     operating, investing, financing, discontinued operations             資、已終止經營業務及所得稅類別。實體
     and income tax categories. Entities are also required to             亦須於財務報表的單一附註中提供有關管
     provide specific disclosures about management-defined                理層界定的表現指標的特定披露。
     performance measures in a single note in the financial
     statements.
